26 FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                          26 金融工具的公允價值
     (a) Fair value of Group’s financial assets and financial                     (a) 本集團經常性以公允價值計量的金融資
         liabilities that are measured at fair value on a recurring                   產及金融負債的公允價值
         basis

          The fair value of financial assets and liabilities are determined as      金融資產及金融負債的公允價值按以下
          follows:                                                                  方式釐定：

          –    The fair value of financial assets and financial liabilities         －   歸類為第一級為於活躍流動市場交易
               classified as Level 1 with standard terms and conditions                 並受標準條款及條件規管之金融資產
               and traded on active liquid markets are determined with                  及金融負債，其公允價值分別參照近
               reference to recent transaction price or quoted prices                   期交易價格或標價（不做調整）釐定；
               (unadjusted) respectively;

          –    The fair value of derivative instruments are estimated using         －   衍生工具的公允價值乃根據折算現金
               discounted cash flow analysis and the applicable yield                   流分析及對非可選衍生工具的存續期
               curve for the duration of the non-applicable derivative;                 適用收益率曲線釐定；

          –    The fair value of unlisted investment funds and unlisted             －   歸類為第二級為包含在以公允價值計
               debt securities included in financial assets at fair value               量且其變動計入損益及以公允價值計
               through profit or loss and at FVOCI classified as Level                  量且其變動計入其他綜合收益的的金
               2 are established by reference to the prices quoted by                   融資產的非上市投資基金及非上市債
               respective fund administrators or by using valuation                     務投資，其公允價值參照各自的基金
               techniques including discounted cash flow method. The                    管理人報價或採用估值技術包括折
               main parameters used include bond prices, interest rates,                現現金流量法。使用的主要參數包括
               foreign exchange rates, prepayment rates, counter party                  債券價格，利率，外匯匯率，提前償還
               credit spreads and others; and                                           率，對方信用利差及其他；及

          –    The Level 3 financial assets, primarily comprises unlisted           －   第三級金融資產主要包括非上市股
               equity securities. Fair values are generally determined                  本證券。公允價值通常使用估值技術
               using valuation techniques, including discounted cash                    確定，包括折現現金流量折算和市場
               flows translation and markets comparison methods.                        比較方法。不可觀察的投入包括折現
               Unobservable inputs include discount rates, comparable                   率，可比公司估值倍數，流動性價差，
               company valuation multiples, liquidity spreads, recent                   類似工具的近期交易價格。估值要求
               transaction prices of similar instruments. The valuation                 管理層對模型的不可觀察輸入做出某
               requires management to make certain assumptions about                    些假設。
               unobservable inputs to the models.

          classified as Level 1, fair value is based on quoted prices                       的金融投資，其公允價值乃基於相同資產
          (unadjusted) for identical assets traded in active market.                        於活躍市場中標價（不做調整）釐定。

          For unlisted debt investments measured at amortised cost                          就分類為第二級的非上市以攤餘成本計
          classified as Level 2, fair value is determined by generally                      量的債務投資，其公允價值按公認定價模
          accepted pricing models including discounted cash flow                            式釐定，包括基於可在市場觀察的輸入數
          technique by using observable market inputs such as market                        據（如市場利息收益率）計算的折算現金
          interest yield.                                                                   流量分析。

          For debt investments measured at amortised cost classified as                     就分類為第三級的以攤餘成本計量的債
          Level 3, fair value is determined by generally accepted pricing                   務投資，其公允價值按公認定價模式釐定，
          models including discounted cash flow technique by using                          包括基於無法觀察的折現率計算的折算
          unobservable discount rates that reflect the credit risk.                         現金流量分析，以反映相關的信用風險。

          The Group purchases reinsurance protection from other                      本集團於日常業務過程中向其他再保險
          reinsurers in the normal course of business in order to limit the          公司購買再保險保障，以限制因不能預期

          exposures. In assessing the credit worthiness of reinsurers, the           再保險公司的信用水平時，本集團會考慮

          Group’s past experience with them.

     (c) Asset and liability matching                                              (c) 資產與負債配比

          is to match the Group’s assets with liabilities on the basis               基準配比本集團的資產與負債。本集團通
          of duration. The Group actively manages its assets using                   過平衡質素、多元組合、資產與負債配比、
          an approach that balances quality, diversification, asset and              流動性與投資回報等方面來積極管理資
          liability matching, liquidity and investment return. The goal of           產。投資過程的目標是在可容忍的風險程
          the investment process is to maximise investment returns at a              度內將投資回報提升至最高水平，同時確
          tolerable risk level, whilst ensuring that the assets and liabilities      保資產與負債按現金流動及期限基準管

               securities since the investment portfolio is well diversified.         易對手信用風險。

               At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  在每個報告日，集團評估以攤餘成本
               financial assets measured at amortised cost, debt                   計量的金融資產、以公允價值計量且
               investments at FVOCI and lease receivables are credit-              其變動計入其他綜合收益的債務投資
               impaired. A financial asset is credit impaired when one             和應收金融租賃款是否信用受損。當
               or more events that have a detrimental impact on the                一個或多個以下事件對金融資產的預
               estimated future cash flows of the financial asset have             期未來現金流造成減少時，該金融資
               occurred.                                                           產的信用受損。

               Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired includes         一個金融資產的信用受損的證據包括
               the following observable data:                                      以下可觀測數據：

               –    Internal credit rating is default grade; or                    －   內部信用評級為損失級；或

               –    The lender gives the borrower concessions for                  －   借款人希望債權人因財政困難做
                    economic or contractual reasons due to the debtor                  出經濟上或合約上的讓步，但債
                    financial difficulties, where such concessions are                 權人通常難以接受此讓步；或
                    normally reluctant to be made by the borrower; or

               –    Significant financial difficulty of the issuer or              －   發債人有顯著的財政困難；或
                    counterparty; or

               –    It becoming probable that the borrower will enter              －   借款人有可能破產或重組；或
                    bankruptcy or financial re-organisation; or

               –    Disappearance of an active market for that financial           －   因財政困難，該金融資產不再在
                    asset because of financial difficulties.                           活躍市場上交易。

               A financial asset that has been renegotiated due to                 通常，由於借款人狀況惡化而重新商
               a deterioration in the borrower’s condition is usually              定的金融資產通常被視為信用受損，
               considered to be credit-impaired unless there is evidence           除非有證據表明無法獲得合同現金流
               that the risk of not receiving contractual cash flows has           的風險已大幅降低，並且沒有其他減
               reduced significantly and there are no other indicators of          值跡象。
               impairment.

                    Resort” to that country, as well as the intention,                     以及反映在公開聲明中的政府和
                    reflected in public statements, of governments and                     機構使用這些機制的意圖，包括
                    agencies to use those mechanisms, including an                         評估這些機制的深度，以及無論

                    capacity to fulfil the required criteria.

               counterparty fails to make contractual payments within 90               支付合同款項時，該金融資產被視為
               days of when they fall due.                                             信用受損。

               Inputs, assumptions and techniques used for estimating                 用於估計減值的輸入值、假設和技
               impairment                                                             術

               The parameters and assumptions involved in ECL model                   預期信用減值損失模型涉及的參數和
               are described below:                                                   假設描述如下：

               For financial assets with or without significant increase in           對於信用風險有或沒有顯著增加的
               credit risk, lifetime or 12 months expected credit losses              金融資產，分別提供整個期限或12個
               are provided respectively. ECL is the result of discounting            月的預期信用減值損失。預期信用減
               the product of Exposure at Default (“EAD”), Probability at             值損失是對違約風險敞口（「EAD 」）       、
               Default (“PD”) and Loss Given Default (“LGD”).                         違 約 概 率（「 P D 」）和 違 約 損 失 率
                                                                                     （「LGD 」）的乘積進行折現的結果。

               EAD: EAD is based on the amounts of the Group expects                  EAD：EAD是 基 於 在 違 約 時，本 集 團
               to be owed at the time of default, over the next 12 months             在 未 來12個 月 內 或 在 剩 餘 期 限 內 預
               or over the remaining lifetime.                                        期被虧欠的金額。

               PD: PD represents the likelihood of a borrower defaulting              PD：PD代 表 借 款 人 在 未 來12個 月 內
               on its financial obligation, either over the next 12 months or         或在債務的剩餘期限內違約的可能
               over the remaining lifetime of the obligation, depending on            性，這取決於金融資產自初始確認以
               whether the financial asset has significant increase in credit         來信用風險是否大幅增加或被評估為
               risk since initial recognition or is assessed to be credit-            上述的信用受損。每個內部信用評級
               impaired as described above. PD for each internal credit               的PD由集團的信用評級中心確定，並
               rating is determined by the Group’s Credit Rating Center               每年審查。
               and is reviewed annually.

               LGD: LGD represents the Group’s expectation of the                     LGD：LGD代表本集團對違約風險的
               extent of loss on default exposure. LGD varies type of                 損失範圍的預期。LGD因金融資產的
               financial asset, type of counterparty, seniority of claim              類 型、交 易 對 手 的 類 型、賠 款 的 優 先
               and availability of collateral or other credit support. LGD            權和抵押品或其他信貸支持的可用性
               is expressed as a percentage loss per unit of exposure at              而 不 同。LGD以EAD的 每 單 位 風 險 的
               EAD. The Group determines LGD taking into consideration                損失百分比表示。本集團在確定LGD
               publications by Basel Committee on Banking Supervision                 時考慮了巴塞爾銀行監管委員會和穆

The Group consists of three major business segments: the                 本集團由三項主要業務分部組成：人壽保險
life insurance business, property and casualty insurance                 業務、財產保險業務及再保險業務。本集團亦
business and reinsurance business. The Group also has other              有其他公司經營投資控股、資產管理、養老保
companies and operations in the areas of investment holding,             險及其他業務。太平人壽（本公司持有75.1%
asset management, pensions and other businesses. The life                股 權 之 附 屬 公 司）運 作 之 人 壽 保 險 分 部 就 其
insurance segment operated by TPL, a 75.1%-owned subsidiary,             毛承保保費額、總資產及盈利能力而言是本
is a significant part of the Group in terms of gross premiums            集團重要之部份。為向投資者提供額外資料
written, total assets and profitability. In order to provide investors   以評估太平人壽之盈利能力及估值，本集團
with additional information to evaluate the profitability and            於年度及中期業績公告內披露太平人壽之內
valuation of TPL, the Group discloses the Embedded Value                 含價值及新業務價值。內含價值包括經調整
and New Business Value of TPL in its Annual and Interim                  股東資產淨值及未來有效業務可為股東創造
Results Announcements. The Embedded Value consists of the                之預期現金流之現值，扣減為支持有效業務
shareholders’ adjusted net worth plus the present value of future        而按照監管要求持有償付資本之成本。新業
expected cash flows to shareholders from the in-force business,          務價值乃指以精算方法評估的在本財務期售
less the costs of holding regulatory solvency capital to support         出的人壽保險新業務所產生的經濟價值。
the in-force business. The New Business Value represents an
actuarially determined estimate of the economic value arising
from the new life insurance business issued during the Period.

The Group’s other business segments (including property and              本集團其他業務分部（包括財產保險、再保險
casualty insurance, reinsurance and pension and group life               及養老及團體人壽保險） （統稱「其他核心業
insurance) (collectively, “Other Core Operations”) continue to           務」）繼續發展良好。為向投資者提供更多有
develop well. To provide investors with further information on           關以上營運的資料，本集團披露集團的總內
these operations, the Group also discloses the Group Embedded            含價值。總內含價值乃定義為其他核心業務
Value. The Group Embedded Value is defined as the Adjusted               的經調整資產淨值，加上太平人壽的內含價
Net Worth of the Other Core Operations plus the Embedded                 值。其他核心業務的經調整資產淨值按香港
Value of TPL. The Adjusted Net Worth of the Other Core                   財務報告準則釐定，並對市價及商譽作出調
Operations is determined by Hong Kong Financial Reporting                整。請注意總內含價值的計算並不包括日後
Standards, with marked-to-market and goodwill adjustments.               任何新業務的估值。
Please note that the Group Embedded Value calculation does

The calculations of Embedded Value and the New Business Value          計算太平人壽的內含價值及新業務價值乃基
of TPL are based on certain assumptions with respect to future         於有關未來經驗之若干假設。故此實際結果
experience. Thus, the actual results could differ significantly from   可能與作出該等計算時之預測有重大差異。
what were envisioned when these calculations were made. In             此外，總內含價值亦基於若干假設，因此不應
addition, the Group Embedded Value is also based on certain            視之為評價及評估本集團業務營運的唯一基
assumptions, and should not be viewed as the only benchmark            準。從投資者角度看，中國太平控股之估值乃
for evaluating and valuing the businesses and operations of the        按照本公司股份於某個別日子之股市價格計
Group. From an investor’s perspective, the valuation of CTIH is        量。於評估中國太平控股股份時，投資者不僅
measured by the stock market price of the Company’s shares             要慮及太平人壽的內含價值及新業務價值和
on any particular day. In valuing CTIH’s shares, investors should      總內含價值，而且亦應考慮到其他多項因素。
take into account not only the Embedded Value and the New              此外，本公司擁有太平人壽之75.1%股權。因
Business Value of TPL and the Group Embedded Value, but also           此，不應把下列所披露之於2024年6月30日之
various other considerations. In addition, TPL is 75.1%-owned          太平人壽內含價值及新業務價值全數作為中
by the Company. The Embedded Value and the New Business                國太平控股的估值。倘若彼等認為該等因素
Value of TPL as at 30 June 2024 as disclosed below should              重要，及對本公司之估值關係重大，投資者務
therefore not be applied 100% in valuing CTIH. Investors are           須特別留意該等因素，及其他支持計算太平
advised to pay particular attention to this factor, as well as the     人壽內含價值及新業務價值計算及總內含價

Report on Review of Interim Financial Information
中期財務資料的審閱報告

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                       審閱工作範圍

We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong                  我們的審閱工作是按照香港會計師公會頒布
Standard on Review Engagements 2410, Review of interim                的 香 港 審 閱 準 則 第2410號「獨 立 核 數 師 對 企
financial information performed by the independent auditor of         業中期財務信息執行審閱」的要求進行的。中
the entity, issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public     期財務報告的審閱工作主要包括向財務會計
Accountants. A review of the interim financial report consists of     負責人進行查詢，執行分析性覆核及其他審
making enquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial      閱程序。由於審閱的範圍遠小於按照香港審
and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review      計準則進行審計的範圍，所以不能保證我們
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit     會注意到在審計中可能會被發現的所有重大
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing          事項。因此，我們不發表審計意見。
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
opinion.


CONCLUSION                                                            結論

Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that           根據我們的審閱，我們沒有注意到任何事項
causes us to believe that the interim financial report of the Group   使 我 們 相 信 於2024年6月30日 的 中 期 財 務 報
as at 30 June 2024 is not prepared, in all material respects, in      告在所有重大方面沒有按照香港會計準則第
accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34, Interim             34號「中期財務報告」編製。
financial reporting.
